Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [148] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的甲基化中枢神经系统肿瘤分类方法，在2801样本参考队列上达到96%准确率，在1104样本独立临床队列上分别达到91类级86%和家族级93%的准确率，相比现有最优方法分别提升约4和5个百分点，具有临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA甲基化谱分析已成为中枢神经系统肿瘤分类的有力手段，但仍面临跨队列可迁移性差、方法论正确性不足以及稳健的多类评估等挑战，亟需更加方法学严谨的机器学习方案。

Method: 采用稀疏随机投影进行降维，结合多项逻辑回归进行分类，并在与广泛使用的参考分类器相同的实验设置下进行评估。

Result: 参考队列上分层3折交叉验证平均准确率96%；独立临床队列上91类级准确率86%，家族级准确率93%；相比现有最优参考值（类级82%、家族级88%）分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法在更强的机器学习方法论基础上，在各评估设置下持续优于现有最优方法，可实质性提高中枢神经系统肿瘤分类的可靠性，对临床诊断中的亚型分配和治疗选择具有直接影响。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [149] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书从经典逼近论、优化和泛化理论出发，系统梳理了过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等现代深度学习理论，构建了一个统一的、以证明为导向的理论叙事框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超越任何单一数学解释，现有文献庞杂且碎片化，缺乏统一的理论视角。作者旨在将分散的研究成果整合为连贯的研究叙事，提供一份严格、完整的现代深度学习理论地图。

Method: 本书采用统一的、以证明为导向的论述方式，按照从经典基础到当代机制的路径展开。每个理论都从三个维度审视：其控制的对象（目标）、使其成立的前提假设、以及其无法解释的现象，从而构建出完整的理论框架。

Result: 形成了一部面向研究人员、研究生和具有数学背景实践者的专著，系统呈现了深度学习理论的当前面貌：强大但不完备，且日益聚焦于学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现这一核心问题。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论正处于一个关键发展阶段，其核心议题已从孤立的理论问题转向对涌现机制的整体理解。本书为这一领域提供了系统性的理论地图和未来的研究方向指引。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [150] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NightVision攻击方法，利用当前商业LLM API仅返回单个logit且禁止logit bias的受限条件下，通过新颖的公共集提示技术和首令牌时间测量，仍能有效恢复隐藏维度、深度和参数量等架构参数。在32个开源LLM上，隐藏维度平均相对误差23%（MoE模型9%），深度和参数量对超过30亿参数模型误差在53%以内，表明现有API限制不足以完全隐藏模型架构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM提供商为保护模型架构隐私，已将API限制为仅返回单个logit并取消logit bias功能，以阻止先前利用top-k logits恢复架构参数的攻击。本文旨在检验当前严格API限制下是否仍能有效恢复模型架构参数，揭示现有API保护措施的不足。

Method: 1）提出公共集提示（common set prompting）技术：使用多个提示对同一输出令牌集合暴露log概率，然后进行谱分析以推断隐藏维度；2）利用端到端首令牌时间（TTFT）测量结合估计的隐藏维度，进一步估计深度和参数量；3）在32个开源LLM上进行实验验证。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上，隐藏维度平均相对误差23%（MoE模型仅9%）；对超过30亿参数的模型，深度和参数量估计误差在53%以内。通过大量消融实验展示了精度随令牌预算和模型属性的变化规律。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制仍不足以完全隐藏底层模型的架构细节，商业LLM提供商需采取更严格的保护措施来防止架构参数泄露。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [151] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个利用多模态数据（铁路道口图像+事故报告等结构化数据）评估铁路道口安全性的AI系统，通过路由微调紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM）管道，能够以0.757的宏F1分数区分高风险/低风险道口，并以0.078的RMSE和0.492的相关性估计FRA安全评分，结果与专家判断一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否仅凭铁路道口的视觉线索稳健评估其安全性，以及引入事故报告等结构化数据是否能提升评估能力，最终构建一个对齐专家意见和FRA安全评分的多模态AI安全评估系统。

Method: 提出了一个概念验证管道，包含数据准备和多模态学习范式；核心采用路由微调紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM）架构，融合图像数据与结构化事故历史数据，输出安全评分与风险等级分类。

Result: 系统在高/低风险道口分类上达到0.757宏F1分数；FRA安全评分估计的RMSE为0.078，相关系数为0.492；定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 证明了结合视觉线索和结构化事故数据可以构建有效的铁路道口安全评估AI系统，为后续安全自动化评估提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [152] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer模型在电子健康记录数据中处理数值的三种编码策略（离散、连续和混合），发现混合token方法（对数值进行分箱后再投影）在精度敏感任务与鲁棒性之间取得最佳平衡，且最优分箱数遵循与数据集大小相关的幂律关系。模型在算术任务上表现出"足够好"的近似计算能力，而非精确算术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在EHR数据中处理数值编码时缺乏系统性比较，不同编码策略（离散、连续、混合）在数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性之间存在权衡，需要明确指导实践选择。

Method: 在真实EHR数据嵌入的合成算术任务和真实临床预测任务中，系统比较了离散编码、连续编码和混合编码三种数值编码策略的性能，并推导了最优分箱数与数据集大小的幂律关系。

Result: 1) 显式建模"值-概念"交互的方法在精度敏感算术任务上表现最好，但受架构限制；2) 混合token方法（分箱后投影）是更鲁棒且广泛适用的方案；3) 最优分箱数服从与数据集大小相关的幂律；4) 模型展现的是"足够好"的近似计算而非精确算术；5) 临床任务中纳入实验室值的收益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: 在实践应用中，鲁棒性和可部署性往往比最大数值精度更重要，混合token方法应作为处理EHR数据中数值编码的实用默认方案。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [153] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge是一个基于临床引导的多任务MRI框架，通过自监督预训练与海马分割、萎缩分类及重建等多任务学习，在AD和MCI诊断中取得最优性能（ADNI 88.17%，OASIS 82.78%），并展现出良好的跨队列泛化能力和概率驱动的机会性筛查可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于MRI的阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆症诊断方法因疾病相关结构变化细微且异质性强而面临挑战，亟需更鲁棒和可扩展的识别框架。

Method: NeuroBridge融合大规模自监督MRI预训练与多任务学习（海马分割、海马萎缩分类、重建），再通过门控融合微调。在ADNI和OASIS队列上评估跨队列迁移、概率分析及机会性筛查。

Result: 在所有分类任务中取得最佳性能：ADNI中AD vs. 认知正常对照准确率88.17%，OASIS中82.78%。在MCI相关及混合诊断设置中提升最大，且展现了强跨队列泛化能力、预测概率与准确率的系统性关联以及概率驱动的机会性筛查可行性。

Conclusion: 临床引导的多任务表示学习优于传统单任务方法，NeuroBridge为痴呆评估和MRI机会性筛查提供了鲁棒且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [154] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP通过将Gaunt张量积推广到自旋加权球谐函数，在保持计算效率的同时恢复了被GTP遗漏的反对称路径，实现了与完整CGTP相当的精度，尤其在手性材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)-等变网络中的Clebsch-Gordan张量积(CGTP)计算复杂度为O(L^6)，限制了可扩展性；最近提出的Gaunt张量积(GTP)降低了复杂度但无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达性不完整。

Method: 将Gaunt张量积从标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)，利用SWSH的代数性质恢复缺失的反对称相互作用，同时保持GTP的渐近效率；构建能自然处理张量积奇偶奇分量的更富表达性的等变基。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等多个基准测试上，SpinGTP达到了与完整CGTP相当的精度；通过显式捕获反对称路径，在手性材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现出更优性能。

Conclusion: SpinGTP提供了一种完整、可扩展且数学严谨的路径，用于大规模3D原子系统模拟中的高阶等变性。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [155] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）在编码智能体强化学习后训练中的基础设施开销，发现冷启动延迟差异高达110倍，百万轨迹场景下工时差达1.8倍，主张将执行环境作为训练系统的一部分进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体强化学习系统将执行基础设施视为背景实现细节，忽视了其在大规模交互式软件部署中带来的巨大开销。本文旨在量化不同执行环境对RL后训练效率的影响，揭示优化执行基础设施的潜力。

Method: 对四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）进行对比研究，测量冷启动延迟，并模拟百万条150步轨迹场景下的人工工时消耗。

Result: 冷启动延迟差异高达110倍；在百万条150步轨迹的规模化场景下，不同执行环境间的工人小时数差异达到1.8倍。

Conclusion: 未来编码智能体强化学习系统应将执行环境作为训练系统本身的一部分进行优化，而非仅仅视为部署的管道工程。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [156] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了条件推理树（CIT）和条件推理森林（CIF）作为top-k特征排名方法在下游预测中的表现，通过基准数据集、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验进行分析。研究发现Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值能在完全置换零假设下控制节点级错误率；CIF在17种分类方法中排名第4，18种回归方法中排名第3；自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时影响最大；森林特征采样可能使信息特征被排除在分裂决策之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推理树和森林通过测试特征后再选择分裂阈值来减少分裂选择偏差，但重复的置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算成本高昂。本文旨在系统评估CIT和CIF作为top-k特征排名方法在下游预测任务中的有效性和效率。

Method: 使用真实数据基准、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验；在固定节点下证明Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值控制节点级错误率；通过消融实验分析自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时的影响；使用稀疏高p模拟实验评估森林特征采样对信息特征包含率的影响。

Result: CIF在17种分类方法（22个数据集）中排名第4，在18种回归方法（8个数据集）中排名第3；关闭自适应停止和精确阈值搜索分别使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍和1.9-10.8倍，而下游评分变化不超过0.011；稀疏高p模拟表明森林特征采样可能使信息特征被排除在分裂决策之外。

Conclusion: 在评估的下游预测基准中，CIF作为top-k特征排名方法表现良好，支持其有效性。但森林特征采样策略在稀疏高维场景下存在信息特征被遗漏的风险。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了MoE模型在领域特定专家剪枝下的效用与可靠性权衡，发现适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且不立即降低可靠性，但极端剪枝会增加幻觉风险，跨域迁移时效用与可靠性均快速下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽能通过选择性激活提升推理速度，但内存需求大；结构化专家剪枝可降低部署成本，但以往研究主要评估基准效用，忽视了对事实可靠性的影响，尤其是在生物医学等高风险领域。

Method: 评估4种MoE模型、6种剪枝方法及多种剪枝比例，在生物医学领域内和跨域场景下，分别在生成和分类任务上测试效用与可靠性。

Result: 适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且无明显可靠性下降，但极端剪枝时幻觉风险增加；迁移至通用领域时，效用与可靠性均快速退化。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖任务和领域；仅基于效用评估剪枝后的MoE模型不足以支撑高风险场景的部署，必须结合可靠性评估。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [158] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出几何感知R-结构KAN网络（GRS-KAN），将R函数融入KAN框架，通过可微逻辑运算编码几何约束，在含间断回归任务中RMSE降低67%并提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KAN虽擅长学习连续非线性结构，但难以显式编码已知的几何/逻辑约束（如间断边界、可行域等），导致复杂几何结构的学习效率低且可解释性不足。

Method: 将V.L.Rvachev的R函数（可微逻辑运算：R-合取/R-析取）集成到KAN分支中，提出加性、乘性和无偏三大变体架构。KAN分支学习平滑非线性，R函数解析编码几何约束，实现端到端可微训练。

Result: 在圆形/矩形支撑的间断回归任务中，GRS-KAN相比标准KAN测试RMSE最高降低67%，边界定位精度显著提升；无偏变体能自动判断几何先验是否有效。

Conclusion: 几何先验的显式解析编码能大幅提升KAN在含间断/几何约束任务中的预测精度与可解释性，R函数提供了一种将逻辑规则可微融入神经架构的有效范式。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [159] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Ember优化器，专门针对语言模型的嵌入表和LM-head矩阵，利用其独特的梯度几何特性，以极低显存开销（O(V+D)而非O(2VD)）在监督微调、强化学习和预训练中提升Pareto前沿，并揭示token优化轨迹可由简单1D射线描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的嵌入表和LM-head作为连续程序与离散符号之间的读写接口，其梯度几何特性与隐藏权重截然不同，但现有优化器（如Adam）未利用这一特性，且显存开销过高（O(2VD)）。

Method: 提出Ember优化器，针对嵌入表和LM-head矩阵设计轻量级优化方案，显存复杂度为O(V+D)；通过分析优化轨迹发现token优化可由1D射线描述，揭示其低维几何结构。

Result: Ember在监督微调、RL和预训练中均能改善Pareto前沿；只需KB级优化器状态；在批大小和参数量上有效扩展；token优化轨迹符合简单1D射线模型。

Conclusion: 嵌入表和LM-head的优化具有特殊的低维几何结构，Ember利用这一特性实现了高效优化；同时提供了对Transformer训练中优化器空间的新的理论视角。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [160] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCGNM客户端优化器，通过类分组动量归一化解决联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，并引入FedHOO算法优化小规模联邦中的重采样率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中类别不平衡导致少数类性能差，且由于隐私和异构性约束无法直接应用中心化技术。

Method: FedCGNM将类别基于最小组内方差分为少量组，维护每组动量，归一化后求和作为更新方向；FedHOO基于X-armed-bandit算法高效优化重采样率。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准和专有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM持续优于基线，FedHOO在小型联邦中带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 类分组动量归一化能有效平衡多数类与少数类梯度幅值并抑制噪声，理论收敛分析支持时变重采样率。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [161] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个将模型规模、训练数据和批次大小拆分为三项的缩放定律（三项定律），能够更高效、更鲁棒地拟合最优批次大小及子优批次下的缩放行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律通常只考虑模型大小和训练数据量，忽略了批次大小对训练效率与最终性能的影响，导致拟合时需要大量最优配置的试验。该研究旨在提出一种包含批次大小的三项缩放定律，以更少的试验资源准确刻画最优批次大小的缩放特性。

Method: 在大量训练运行数据上拟合提出的三项缩放定律（显式将训练步骤和批次大小分离），通过与传统两项定律对比验证其恢复最优批次大小缩放的能力，并利用子优批次训练数据增强拟合的鲁棒性。

Result: 三项定律能够正确恢复最优批次大小的缩放规律；由于利用了子优批次大小的训练运行，所需训练运行数量显著减少；该定律可推导子优批次下的缩放行为，且与先前关于临界批次大小的实证结果一致。

Conclusion: 提出的三项缩放定律更全面地描述了模型规模、数据量与批次大小之间的权衡关系，降低了实验成本，并为子优批次设置下的性能预测提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [162] [Don't Let Gains FADE: Breaking Down Policy Gradient Weights in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01490)
*Juliette Decugis,Sean O'Brien,Francis Bach,Gabriel Synnaeve,Taco Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FADE（Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy），一种能根据训练动态自动调度梯度权重的自适应性优势函数，通过统一框架分解任意优势函数的正负梯度质量和难度轴，解决了强化学习后训练中LLM推理的训练不稳定性和多样性崩溃问题。FADE在7B和32B规模上分别比最佳静态基线早20k和2k步达到峰值pass@1，并在LiveCodeBench和AIME上实现最佳准确率-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练能显著提升LLM推理能力，但存在训练不稳定性和多样性崩溃两大问题。虽然优势函数提供了一种修复方案，但众多方法使得"使用何种优势、何时使用"变得混乱不清，缺乏统一的理论指导。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将任意优势函数沿两个正交轴（符号轴和难度轴）分解为正负梯度质量；基于此分析训练动态中探索与利用阶段的权衡（探索期需要平衡和硬焦点，利用期需要抑制和中焦点），设计FADE自适应优势函数，根据训练动态自动调度梯度权重。

Result: FADE在7B规模上比最佳静态基线早20k步达到峰值pass@1，在32B规模上早2k步；在LiveCodeBench和AIME上所有pass@k指标均取得最佳准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架和FADE方法有效解决了强化学习后训练中的不稳定性和多样性崩溃问题，实现了更高效的训练收敛和更优的准确率-多样性平衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training dramatically improves LLM reasoning, but suffers from training instability and diversity collapse. Advantage functions offer an appealing fix: they reshape the training objective, reweight which rollouts drive learning, and are trivial to implement. Yet a proliferation of methods makes it unclear which advantage to use and when. We cut through the confusion with a unifying framework that decomposes any advantage into its positive and negative gradient mass along two orthogonal axes. On the sign axis, imbalanced updates collapse either entropy or weight geometry. On the difficulty axis, hard-problem focus sharpens signal but costs sample size. Both trade-offs shift during training: exploration favors balance and hard focus; exploitation favors suppression and medium focus. This motivates FADE (Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy), a self-adapting advantage that reads training dynamics to schedule the gradient weight automatically. FADE reaches peak pass@1 20k steps earlier than the best static baseline at the 7B scale and 2k steps earlier at the 32B , while achieving the best accuracy-diversity trade-off across all pass@k on LiveCodeBench and AIME.

</details>


### [163] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式学习中拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私和泛化误差之间的权衡关系，发现隐私与泛化误差的关系取决于隐私强度：高噪声（强隐私）下隐私减少泛化误差，低噪声（弱隐私）下隐私增加泛化误差，并通过算法稳定性理论给出了匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作建立了分布式学习中拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私和优化误差之间的三元困境，本文试图探究这一困境是否同样适用于泛化误差，并揭示隐私强度如何影响该权衡关系。

Method: 通过算法稳定性分析，推导了LDP约束下拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习的泛化误差上下界，分别针对高噪声（强隐私）和低噪声（弱隐私）两种隐私体制进行理论刻画，并辅以实验验证。

Result: 高噪声（强隐私）体制下，增强隐私反而降低泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私无冲突；低噪声（弱隐私）体制下，隐私与鲁棒性之间的紧张关系重现，增强隐私会损害泛化性能。泛化误差随隐私强度呈现非单调变化。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性、LDP和泛化误差之间的三元困境并非普遍成立，其表现取决于隐私体制，这为设计既鲁棒又保护隐私的分布式学习算法提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [164] [Towards Learning Representations of Policies in Two-Player Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01498)
*Kevin Wang,Kevin Yang,Arjun Prakash,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究两人零和有限信息博弈中策略表示（嵌入）的学习问题，提出了策略数据集构建方法、策略表示学习方法以及下游评估任务，并在Kuhn和Leduc扑克上验证了基础方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在两人零和有限信息博弈中，学习有用的策略表示（嵌入）是一个尚未被充分研究的问题，现有工作缺乏对不同自监督学习方法的系统性比较。

Method: 1) 提出多种构建策略数据集的方法；2) 提出多种学习策略表示（嵌入）的方法；3) 设计下游任务用于评估所学表示的有效性。

Result: 在Kuhn扑克和Leduc扑克上对所提方法进行评估，尽管方法较为基础，但实验表明学到的嵌入中包含了有用的行为表示。

Conclusion: 该工作是首批系统比较自监督学习方法用于学习博弈策略表示的研究之一，为后续工作提供了基准和开源代码。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of learning useful policy representations (embeddings) in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. We make three contributions: First, we introduce methods of creating datasets of policies for a given game. Second, we propose methods to learn policy representations. Third, we introduce downstream tasks to evaluate the effectiveness of such representations.
  We evaluate each dataset method, embedding method, and downstream task on Kuhn and Leduc Poker. Although our methods are very basic, we demonstrate that useful behavioral representations are present in the learned embeddings. To our knowledge, this work is among the first to systematically compare self-supervised learning techniques for learning policy representations in games. Our code is available at https://github.com/VitamintK/ssl-project for others to extend.

</details>


### [165] [The risk of KV cache compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01520)
*Lukas Haverbeck,Carmen Amo Alonso,Andres Felipe Posada-Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度刻画了KV缓存压缩的极小化极大风险，揭示了缓存内在可压缩性决定压缩可行性的条件，并提出了在因果遮蔽下达到极小化极大最优风险的设计原则，最终实例化为实用算法并在LongBench上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法主要依赖经验实验，缺乏理论指导；虽然理论上已证明最坏情况下压缩不可能，但未系统指导在可压缩场景下的算法设计。

Method: 通过刻画KV缓存压缩的极小化极大风险，建立压缩可行性与缓存内在可压缩性之间的理论联系；基于此提出因果遮蔽下映射到prefill和自回归解码的设计原则，并实例化为实用算法。

Result: 理论结果揭示了何时以及如何实现准确压缩；实例化算法在LongBench上取得有前景的性能表现。

Conclusion: 为具有理论保证的实用KV缓存压缩提供了原则性路径，弥合了理论与实践之间的差距。

Abstract: Transformer inference on long sequences is expensive because softmax attention repeatedly reads from a large KV cache. The prevalent approach to this bottleneck is KV cache compression, which replaces the full cache with a compact summary. Despite its practical importance, the design of such summaries is largely driven by empirical experimentation. On the theoretical side, existing results show that KV cache compression can be impossible in the worst case, but offer little systematic guidance for designing algorithms in regimes where accurate compression is possible. We bridge this gap by characterizing the minimax risk of KV cache compression in terms of the intrinsic compressibility of a cache, revealing when and how accurate compression is possible. These results yield novel design principles for KV cache compression under causal masking that map efficiently to prefill and autoregressive decoding while achieving minimax-optimal risk. We instantiate these principles in a practical algorithm and report promising performance on LongBench in targeted experiments. Overall, our results provide a principled avenue for practical KV cache compression with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-Head Recurrent Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01523)
*Jiatong Li,Samuel Yeh,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文诊断了递归记忆智能体在长上下文场景中性能退化的根本原因——记忆保留瓶颈，并提出了一种无需训练的通用框架——多头递归记忆（MHM），通过划分独立记忆头并采用“先选择后更新”策略，从根本上防止覆盖问题。轻量级实例MHM-LRU在100K-1M token范围内显著提升了记忆保留率和端到端准确率，在896K token上记忆保留率从不足30%提升至73.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归记忆智能体虽然通过将输入迭代合并到固定大小的记忆窗口来扩展LLM的上下文长度，但其端到端性能随上下文增长而系统性退化，存在可靠性问题。现有工作未深入分析这种退化的根本原因，作者希望通过对性能进行分解诊断，找出关键瓶颈并设计针对性的解决方案。

Method: 1. 将性能分解为记忆捕获（memory capture）和记忆保留（memory retention）两个因素，定量确认保留是主导瓶颈。2. 提出Multi-Head Recurrent Memory (MHM)，将记忆划分为多个独立头，采用阶段式的select-then-update策略，每次只更新一个头，其余头从结构上防止被覆盖。3. 提出轻量级实例MHM-LRU，使用Least-Recently-Updated策略选择更新头，保证均匀利用且零额外token开销。

Result: 在长上下文基准测试上，MHM-LRU在100K-1M token范围内显著提升了记忆保留率和端到端准确率。在RULER-HQA的896K token设置下，记忆保留率从不到30%提升至73.96%。该增益在不同模型家族、模型规模和任务类型上均表现出一致性。

Conclusion: 记忆保留瓶颈源于现有递归记忆设计将记忆作为单一文本块进行维护，每次更新都可能覆盖已有内容。通过架构优化——将记忆划分为独立头并采用选择性更新策略——可以可靠地解决这一问题。这为长上下文递归记忆提供了一条实用且成本高效的路径。

Abstract: Recurrent memory agents extend LLMs to arbitrarily long contexts by iteratively consolidating input into a fixed-size memory window. Despite their scalability, these agents exhibit a well-documented reliability problem: end-to-end performance degrades systematically as context length grows. We diagnose this failure by decomposing performance into two factors--memory capture and memory retention--and quantitatively confirm that retention is the dominant bottleneck. Retention collapses because existing designs maintain memory as a monolithic text block, forcing every update to risk overwriting previously retained content. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Multi-Head Recurrent Memory (MHM), a general, training-free framework that partitions memory into independent heads governed by a stage-wise select-then-update strategy. At each step, exactly one head is selected for update while the remaining heads are structurally shielded from overwriting, shifting the burden of retention from model behavior to architectural design. As a lightweight instantiation, we introduce Least-Recently-Updated MHM (MHM-LRU), which guarantees uniform head utilization with zero additional token overhead. Extensive experiments on long-context benchmarks show that MHM-LRU substantially improves both retention and end-to-end accuracy across the 100K--1M token range, where baselines degrade sharply. On RULER-HQA at 896K tokens, MHM-LRU improves the memory retention rate from less than 30% to 73.96%. These gains generalize across model families, scales, and task types, positioning architectural optimization as a practical and cost-efficient path toward reliable long-context recurrent memory.

</details>


### [167] [Wind-Aware Reinforcement Learning Control of a Small Quadrotor Using Learned Onboard Wind Estimation in Simulated Atmospheric Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01528)
*Abdullah Al Tasim,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段学习框架：首先用注意力增强的GRU网络从机载运动学与动力学数据中估计局部风矢量（RMSE 0.40 m/s，方向误差3.2°），然后将该估计输入PPO强化学习控制器，使水平轨迹跟踪误差较无风感知的PD基线降低48%，在强风中风感知贡献约占一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型多旋翼飞行器在大气边界层中运行时，湍流风速与飞行器空速相当，会严重降低轨迹跟踪精度甚至使传统反馈控制失效，因此需要有效的在线风估计与抗风控制方法。

Method: 两阶段学习管线：(1) 风估计器：注意力增强的GRU网络，在数千次含Von Kármán湍流、幂律风切变与偏转的模拟飞行数据上训练，从机载运动学与动力学中恢复水平风矢量；(2) 飞行控制器：PPO强化学习控制器，接收冻结的风估计器输出作为状态输入。

Result: 风估计器在未见风况下逐飞行RMSE为0.40 m/s，方向误差3.2°；垂直上升剖面技能评分0.861。PPO控制器在4-12 m/s平均风速下水平轨迹跟踪误差较无风PD基线降低48%，100%的评估回合获胜；三路消融显示风感知贡献随风速增大，在强风中约占总体收益的一半；在13-15 m/s分布外风况下基线彻底失败而所提控制器性能优雅退化。

Conclusion: 基于学习的在线风估计与强化学习控制器的结合能显著提升多旋翼在湍流边界层中的轨迹跟踪性能，风感知的贡献随风速增大而增大，且该方法对超出训练分布的强风具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Small multirotor aircraft are increasingly tasked with operations in the atmospheric boundary layer, where turbulent winds comparable to the vehicle's airspeed degrade trajectory tracking and can defeat conventional feedback control. This work illustrates a two-stage learning pipeline that first estimates the local wind from onboard kinematics and dynamics and then exploits that estimate inside a reinforcement learning (RL) flight controller. The wind estimator, an attention-augmented gated recurrent network trained on thousands of simulated flights through von Karman turbulence with power-law shear and veer, recovers the horizontal wind vector with a per-flight root-mean-square error of 0.40 m/s and a direction error of 3.2 degrees on unseen wind regimes, an accuracy near the floor imposed by unresolved turbulence, and generalizes to vertical ascent profiles with a skill score of 0.861 over a constant-wind reference. A proximal policy optimization controller receiving the frozen estimator's output reduces horizontal trajectory tracking error by 48% relative to a wind-blind proportional-derivative baseline across mean winds of 4 m/s to 12 m/s, winning on 100% of evaluation episodes. A three-way ablation decomposes this improvement into a kinematic component, available without wind information, and a wind-perception component; the perception share rises with wind speed, from small in light winds toward roughly half the total benefit in strong winds, consistent with the quadratic scaling of aerodynamic drag. The controller degrades gracefully on out-of-distribution winds of 13 m/s to 15 m/s, where the baseline fails catastrophically.

</details>


### [168] [Certified World Models as Sensing Clocks: Drift-Aware Deadlines for Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01537)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于认证世界模型的感知时钟机制，利用模型的有效性水平线来指导智能体在何时进行重新感知，并证明了在部署时需要考虑漂移因素，通过校准的原生滚动漂移包络来保证可靠性。在VN-JEPA模型和合成基准测试中验证了该方法在控制证书违反和减少事件尾部违规方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决认证世界模型在部署时预测有效性估计不准的问题，传统的流形上Lyapunov率会高估无感知区间的有效性，需要一种漂移感知的部署方法。

Method: 从经过审计的等变世界模型出发，推导无感知区间的截止期限；提出漂移感知的部署方法，使用校准的原生滚动漂移包络替代流形上Lyapunov率；在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型和线索条件定理台（合成基准）上验证。

Result: 在冻结VN-JEPA模型上，感知时钟在跨种子和数据分片的搁置区间同时证书违反控制上有效；在合成基准上，时钟在部署分布上保持有效，并在匹配感知预算下显著减少事件尾部违规。在短视界冻结VN-JEPA设定中，经验共形水平线与部署时钟在有效性和预算上匹配。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个认证的感知时钟原语和漂移感知部署方法，而非声称谱时钟在经验上优于所有非谱调度器。

Abstract: Certified world models estimate how long their predictions remain valid. We turn this validity horizon into an operational sensing clock: a rule for when an agent should stop coasting and re-sense. Starting from an audited equivariant world model, we derive a deadline for no-sensing intervals and show that deployable deadlines in learned world models must be drift-aware: on-manifold Lyapunov rates alone overestimate coasting validity, while calibrated native rollout-drift envelopes carry the deployed guarantee. On a frozen 3D VN-JEPA model, the resulting clock controls held-out interval-simultaneous certificate violation across seeds and data shards. In a cue-conditioned theorem-bed (a synthetic bench where all schedulers share the exact model, isolating the scheduling rule), the clock remains valid on the deployment distribution and substantially reduces eventful-tail violations relative to exact-mixture expected-belief scheduling at matched sensing budget. We also report limits: in the short-horizon frozen VN-JEPA regime, empirical conformal horizons match the deployed clock on validity and budget, and a partial-reset exploration finds no clean budget-matched advantage for the spectral term. Thus the contribution is a certified sensing-clock primitive and drift-aware deployment method, not a claim that spectral clocks empirically dominate all non-spectral schedulers.

</details>


### [169] [Evolutionary Feature Engineering for Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01548)
*Ege Onur Taga,Yilin Zhuang,M. Emrullah Ildiz,Petros Mol,Abhimanyu Das,Karthik Duraisamy,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EFE框架，利用大语言模型驱动的进化算法自动发现结构化数据的预处理变换，在时间序列预测和表格预测两个场景中分别提升预测精度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽已被用于进化优化中的搜索算子，但在结构化数据的特征工程领域，如何利用LLM自动发现有效的预处理变换仍是一个开放问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一种既能提升预测精度又能保持可解释性的自动化特征工程方法。

Method: 将预处理变换表示为具有标准fit/transform接口的Python程序，通过LLM驱动的进化算法进行优化。进化过程中利用数据集上下文、摘要统计信息和验证集上的下游性能反馈来迭代改进候选程序。在时间序列场景（EFE-Time）中学习可逆的、数据集特定的归一化变换；在表格预测场景（EFE-Tab）中进化出紧凑的特征程序以添加有用特征并移除冗余特征。

Result: EFE-Time在多个时间序列数据集上将预测误差（MASE、WQL、MAE）平均降低3%以上，在COVID-Deaths数据集上最高降低19%，且对Chronos-2等最新TSFM有效。EFE-Tab在表格预测中达到或超越现有基于LLM的特征工程方法，尤其在使用经典决策树时，少量进化特征即可获得有竞争力的精度并保持可解释性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的进化方法能够同时提升结构化数据任务的准确性和可解释性，EFE框架为自动化特征工程提供了一种有效的范式。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as open-ended search operators in evolutionary optimization. We introduce Evolutionary Feature Engineering (EFE), a framework for using LLM-based evolution to discover preprocessing transformations for structured data. EFE represents transformations as Python programs with a standardized fit/transform interface, allowing them to be inserted directly into existing machine learning pipelines. During evolution, candidate programs are refined using dataset context, summary statistics, and downstream performance feedback on validation set. We instantiate EFE in two settings. For time-series forecasting, EFE-Time learns invertible, dataset-specific normalizations that improve off-the-shelf time-series foundation models. It reduces forecasting errors (MASE, WQL, MAE) 3% or more when averaged across datasets and improvements are as much as 19% on the COVID-Deaths dataset. Notably, these improvements occur with recent TSFMs such as Chronos-2. For tabular prediction, EFE-Tab evolves compact feature programs that add useful interpretable features and remove redundant ones, improving or matching existing LLM-based feature-engineering methods. We found EFE-Tab to be particularly effective on classical decision trees, where small sets of evolved features yield competitive accuracy while preserving interpretability. Overall, EFE demonstrates that LLM-based evolution can improve both accuracy and interpretability when automatically tackling structured data.

</details>


### [170] [X-LogSMask: Expand Transformer for Graph-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01553)
*Leyan Li,Rennong Yang,Zhenxing Zhang,Liping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-LogSMask，一种可解释的多头对数结构掩码，通过将对称归一化图拓扑直接注入注意力logits，使标准Transformer能够有效处理图数据，无需改变架构。在20个基准测试中，该方法在13个数据集上达到SOTA，并在一层轻量配置下保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的全对全自注意力机制与图数据的稀疏、结构化、多尺度交互不匹配。现有图Transformer通过结构编码、混合消息传递或学习注意力约束来缓解这一问题，但这些方法通常引入额外复杂性和有限的可解释性。

Method: 提出X-LogSMask，一种可解释的多头对数结构掩码。它将对称归一化图拓扑直接注入注意力logits，利用对数变换将结构连通性转换为拓扑感知的门控信号，抑制不支持节点交互同时保留特征依赖注意力。通过为不同注意力头分配归一化邻接矩阵的不同幂次，使每个头具有定义的结构半径，支持单层内的多跳信息传播。

Result: 在20个节点级、边级和图级基准测试中，配备X-LogSMask的Transformer在13个数据集上达到SOTA，且在轻量单层配置下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单、可解释的结构掩码可以使自注意力成为有效的图学习算子，且无需改变Transformer架构。

Abstract: Transformers have become general-purpose architectures, but their all-to-all self-attention is poorly matched to graph data, whose interactions are sparse, structured and multi-scale. Existing Graph Transformers address this mismatch through structural encodings, hybrid message-passing modules or learned attention constraints, often introducing additional complexity and limited interpretability. Here we introduce X-LogSMask, an explainable multi-head logarithmic structural mask that injects symmetrically normalized graph topology directly into attention logits. The logarithmic transform converts structural connectivity into a topology-aware gating signal, suppressing unsupported node interactions while preserving feature-dependent attention. By assigning different powers of the normalized adjacency matrix to different attention heads, X-LogSMask gives each head a defined structural radius and supports multi-hop information propagation within a single layer. We further show that a standard Transformer encoder can be interpreted as one-step message passing on a complete graph, motivating X-LogSMask as a topology-constrained alternative to unrestricted self-attention. Across 20 node-, edge- and graph-level benchmarks, Transformers equipped with X-LogSMask achieve state-of-the-art performance on 13 datasets and remain competitive in a lightweight one-layer configuration. These results show that simple, interpretable structural masks can make self-attention an effective graph-learning operator without changing the Transformer architecture. The code is available at https://github.com/LiLeyan-0120/X-LogSMask.

</details>


### [171] [Geometric Signatures of Reasoning: A Spectral Perspective on Task Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01571)
*Aria Masoomi,Mahsa Bazzaz,Adel Javanmard,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型链式思维推理轨迹在隐藏状态空间中的几何特性，提出了有效维度d_ρ作为轨迹复杂度的度量，并证明平坦特征谱对应更难任务；同时发现运动学特征可在仅生成20% token时预测答案正确性，d_ρ在MATH500数据集上区分难易问题AUC达0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注推理链的长度和内容，但对其内部几何结构知之甚少，作者希望通过分析CoT轨迹的几何特性来揭示任务难度与答案正确性。

Method: 将每条推理链形式化为R^d空间中的离散曲线，通过谱、位置和运动学三类几何泛函进行刻画；提出有效维度d_ρ衡量轨迹复杂度，并从理论上证明特征谱平坦度与任务难度的关系；利用运动学特征（平均位置、位置离散度、速度、速度离散度等）预测答案正确性。

Result: 在MATH500数学推理数据集上，d_ρ区分难易问题的AUC达0.93；运动学特征仅需前20%的生成token即可预测正确性，且该正确性信号可跨不同难度问题迁移。

Conclusion: 模型内部推理轨迹的形状是理解任务难度和答案质量的原理性窗口，运动学特征可用于早期停止策略。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables large language models (LLMs) to solve complex problems by generating intermediate reasoning steps. While much attention has been paid to the length and content of these reasoning chains, far less is known about their internal geometry. We study the \emph{geometry} of CoT trajectories in the hidden state space of transformer models, formalizing each reasoning chain as a discrete curve in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and characterizing it through spectral, positional, and kinematic geometric functionals. We introduce the effective dimension $d_ρ$ as a measure of trajectory complexity and show theoretically that trajectories with flatter eigenvalue spectra correspond to harder tasks, as they explore more of the hidden dimensions. Lastly, we explore how kinematic features of the trajectory, mean position, positional dispersion, initial and current hidden states, mean velocity, mean speed, and speed dispersion, can be used to predict solution correctness before generation is complete, and may inform future early-stopping strategies. Experimentally, on mathematical reasoning problems from the MATH500 dataset, $d_ρ$ achieves $0.93$ AUC in distinguishing easy from hard problems, while kinematic features potentially can predict correctness from only the first $20\%$ of generated tokens. These correctness signatures transfer across questions of varying difficulty, establishing that the shape of a model's internal reasoning trajectory is a principled window into both task hardness and solution quality.

</details>


### [172] [BOUNDARY_SYNC: Measuring Communication-Induced Representational Coupling in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01600)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BOUNDARY_SYNC协议，通过耦合放大因子（CAF）量化LLM智能体间通信导致的表征耦合，发现文本通信导致显著同质化（CAF=0.803），图像通信亦有类似效应，且群组大小可调节耦合方向（K=5同质化 vs K=3多样化），耦合主要由提示上下文驱动而非累积更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被部署为通信智能体，智能体间通信是否会导致输出趋同（同质化）或分化（多样化）这一问题尚待解答，作者旨在量化并理解这一耦合现象。

Method: 提出BOUNDARY_SYNC协议，定义耦合放大因子CAF = JSD_cond / JSD_baseline（CAF<1同质化，CAF>1多样化）；在GPT-4o上进行受控实验（N=30，约9,900次API调用），测量文本和图像通信下的表征耦合，并设置无通信消融和提示扰动对照。

Result: (1) 文本通信显著同质化（CAF=0.803, d=1.30, p<0.001）；(2) 图像通信在模态内基线下同质化（CAF=0.834）；(3) 群组大小调节方向：K=5同质化，K=3趋向多样化（CAF>1.0）；(4) 跨模型复制显示极端变异（CAF 0.034-0.803）；(5) 耦合是无状态的，由提示上下文驱动而非累积更新，连续共识产生单调收敛。

Conclusion: LLM智能体耦合是真实、可测量且可在提示层面控制的，对多智能体系统设计具有直接意义。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as communicating agents, does inter-agent communication cause outputs to converge? We introduce BOUNDARY_SYNC, a protocol measuring representational coupling via the Coupling Amplification Factor (CAF = JSD_cond / JSD_baseline), where CAF < 1 indicates homogenization and CAF > 1 indicates diversification. In controlled GPT-4o experiments (N=30, ~9,900 API calls), we measure coupling in text and image communication. Key findings: (1) text communication causes significant homogenization (CAF=0.803 [0.740, 0.873], d=1.30, p<0.001), confirmed by no-communication ablation and prompt-perturbation controls; (2) image communication also homogenizes under within-modality baselines (CAF=0.834 [0.811, 0.858]), with comparable proportional effect; (3) group size moderates coupling direction -- K=5 produces homogenization while K=3 yields CAF > 1.0 (point estimates 1.14 and 1.06, CI pending), suggesting a directional shift toward diversification; (4) cross-model replication shows extreme variation (CAF 0.034-0.803), with DeepSeek dominated by format artifacts; (5) coupling is stateless -- driven by prompt context rather than cumulative updating, with continuous consensus producing monotonic convergence. These results establish LLM agent coupling as real, measurable, and controllable at the prompt level, with direct implications for multi-agent system design.

</details>


### [173] [SINA: A Fully Automated Circuit Schematic Image to Netlist Generator Using Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01609)
*Saoud Aldowaish,Yashwanth Karumanchi,Kai-Chen Chiang,Mohammed Ayman Habib,Finn Murphy,Rishen Cao,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出开源电路原理图图像到网表的自动生成工具SINA，集成深度学习组件检测、连通分量标注连接推断、OCR提取参考标识符、VLM分配参考标识符，支持IC和PCB级原理图，含交叉线检测，网表生成准确率96.67%，比现有方法高2.72倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI/LLM在模拟和混合信号电路设计中的应用受限于缺乏机器可读的电路设计知识表示。大量电路原理图以图像形式存在于文献、教材和网站中，无法直接被EDA工具处理，而现有转换方法缺乏通用性，在组件识别、连接推断以及区分连接节点与交叉线方面存在困难。

Method: SINA是一个全自动流水线，集成(1)深度学习用于鲁棒的组件检测，(2)连通分量标注用于精确的连接推断，(3)OCR用于组件参考标识符提取，(4)视觉语言模型(VLM)用于可靠的参考标识符分配。同时包含专用交叉线检测模块，并采用图同构技术验证生成网表的正确性。

Result: 整体网表生成准确率达到96.67%，比现有最先进方法高2.72倍。

Conclusion: SINA作为一个开源原理图图像到网表的自动化生成工具，能够有效处理IC和PCB级原理图，显著优于现有方法，为EDA工具中模拟和混合信号电路设计知识的利用提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have revolutionized Electronic Design Automation (EDA), particularly through Large Language Models (LLMs) for circuit design tasks. However, their application to analog and mixed-signal domains remains limited by the lack of machine-readable representations of existing circuit design knowledge. Circuit schematic images found in research manuscripts, textbooks, and websites constitute a vast repository of validated designs; however, these visual representations cannot be directly processed by EDA tools. Converting them into machine-readable netlists is essential for enabling simulation, verification, and building comprehensive databases for AI-based models. Current conversion methods lack generalization across both Integrated Circuit (IC) and Printed Circuit Board (PCB) level schematics. Moreover, they struggle with component recognition and connectivity inference, and fail to distinguish between connected junctions and crossing wires. In this paper, we propose SINA, an open-source circuit schematic image-to-netlist generator. SINA is a fully automated pipeline that integrates deep learning for robust component detection, connected-component labeling for accurate connectivity inference, Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for component reference designator extraction, and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for reliable reference designator assignment. SINA handles both IC- and PCB-level schematics and incorporates dedicated crossing-wires detection to differentiate wire intersections from connections. We validate the correctness of the generated netlists using graph isomorphism techniques. Our experiments demonstrate an overall netlist generation accuracy of 96.67%, which is 2.72x higher compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [174] [MKGR: Multimodal Knowledge-Graph Representation Learning for Cold-Start Protein-Protein Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01627)
*Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\method的多模态表示框架，用于冷启动蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用（PPI）预测。该框架结合了区域感知的蛋白质序列编码和四种生物医学知识图谱（蛋白质-药物、蛋白质-疾病、蛋白质-miRNA、蛋白质-lncRNA关联）。序列分支从结构信息指导的序列区域提取上下文表示，图注意力编码器从稀疏生物医学关联中学习模态特异的蛋白质嵌入。桥梁重建目标通过恢复共享的蛋白质-实体关联来正则化图学习，对级门控模块自适应整合序列和图证据。在两个基准数据集上的新颖-旧有和新颖-新颖冷启动设置下，\method在ACC、F1、AUC、AUPR和MCC指标上持续优于有竞争力的序列、网络和知识图谱基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用（PPI）预测对功能基因组学、疾病机制发现和药物开发至关重要。当候选相互作用包含训练中未见过的蛋白质（即冷启动场景）时，仅依赖网络拓扑的模型会失去有用的上下文信息，预测性能显著下降。现有方法难以有效处理这种冷启动PPI预测问题。

Method: \method框架包含四个核心组件：1) 序列分支：从结构信息指导的序列区域提取上下文表示；2) 图注意力编码器：从四种生物医学知识图谱（蛋白质-药物、蛋白质-疾病、蛋白质-miRNA、蛋白质-lncRNA）学习模态特异的蛋白质嵌入；3) 桥梁重建目标：通过恢复共享的蛋白质-实体关联来正则化图学习；4) 对级门控模块：自适应整合序列和图证据，用于每对候选蛋白质的预测。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的新颖-旧有和新颖-新颖冷启动设置下，\method在ACC、F1、AUC、AUPR和MCC五个评估指标上均持续优于有竞争力的序列、网络和知识图谱基线方法。

Conclusion: \method通过多模态融合（蛋白质序列编码+四种生物医学知识图谱）有效解决了冷启动PPI预测问题，证明了在缺乏已知PPI网络拓扑信息时，整合多种生物医学关联知识能够显著提升预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate protein-protein interaction (PPI) prediction is central to functional genomics, disease mechanism discovery, and drug development. A difficult setting arises when candidate interactions include proteins that have no observed PPI edges during training, where models relying on network topology alone often lose useful context. This paper presents \method, a multimodal representation framework for cold-start PPI prediction. \method\ combines region-aware protein sequence encoding with four protein-centered biomedical knowledge graphs, including protein-drug, protein-disease, protein-miRNA, and protein-lncRNA associations. The sequence branch extracts contextual representations from structurally informed sequence regions, while graph attention encoders learn modality-specific protein embeddings from sparse biomedical associations. A bridge reconstruction objective regularizes graph learning by recovering shared protein-entity associations, and a pair-level gating module adaptively integrates sequence and graph evidence for each candidate protein pair. Experiments on two benchmark datasets under novel-old and novel-novel cold-start settings show that \method\ consistently outperforms competitive sequence, network, and knowledge-graph baselines across ACC, F1, AUC, AUPR, and MCC.

</details>


### [175] [DeadPool: Resilient LLM Training with Hot-Swapping via Zero-Overhead Checkpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01646)
*Haotian Xie,Junlin Chen,Mingkai Zheng,Lishan Yang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeadPool提出一种基于热插拔的容错机制，通过非关键路径内存检查点和通信器重建协议，实现LLM训练中零开销的无故障执行和极低恢复延迟（<40秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练容错机制在无故障时存在非平凡开销，或在永久节点故障场景下恢复延迟过长，无法同时优化两个目标。

Method: 1) 非关键路径内存检查点实现空间冗余，与计算高效重叠；2) 通信器重建协议，运行时用备用节点替换故障节点，实现热插拔。

Result: 在512 NVIDIA A100 GPU和65B参数LLM上评估，零检查点开销，热插拔恢复在40秒内完成。

Conclusion: DeadPool同时实现了零开销的无故障执行和极低的恢复成本，打破了二者间的权衡。

Abstract: State-of-the-art large language model (LLM) training takes tens of thousands of graphics processing units (GPUs) for months and encounters failures across the software and hardware stack. Existing fault-tolerance mechanisms either impose non-trivial overhead during failure-free execution or suffer from prolonged recovery latency, particularly under scenarios where a small subset of compute nodes experience permanent failures. %The tradeoff between failure-free overhead and recovery latency forms a space forms a Pareto frontier We present DeadPool to simultaneously address both optimization objectives. DeadPool incorporates a fault-tolerance mechanism that restores LLM training via hot-swapping, namely by replacing failed nodes with spare nodes without terminating the complete job. The hot-swapping of DeadPool is enabled by two ideas: First, it exploits an off-critical-path in-memory checkpointing mechanism for spatial redundancy. Second, it introduces a communicator reconstruction protocol that replaces failed nodes with spare nodes at runtime. DeadPool efficiently overlaps the in-memory checkpointing with computation, thus introducing zero overhead during error-free execution. Upon permanent node failures, DeadPool can rebuild memory states with minimal recomputation by leveraging in-memory checkpoints. We evaluate DeadPool across scales (up to 512 NVIDIA A100 GPUs) and LLMs (up to 65B parameters), and observe zero checkpoint overhead with hot-swapping recovery completing in under 40 seconds. These results show that DeadPool simultaneously achieves both zero-overhead error-free execution and extremely low recovery cost.

</details>


### [176] [CALM: Interpretable Cross-Modal Alignment for Biomarker Discovery from Unpaired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01656)
*Jueqi Wang,Zachary Jacokes,John Darrell Van Horn,Kevin A. Pelphrey,Michael C. Schatz,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM是一个能够从完全不重叠的单模态数据（神经影像和遗传学）中学习脑区（ROI）与遗传通路之间可解释关联的框架，通过在共享潜空间中对齐两种模态，实现跨模态交互分析，并在自闭症谱系障碍研究中发现了与已有文献一致的免疫和代谢通路-皮层区域关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑结构与遗传影响之间的相互作用是理解神经精神疾病的关键，然而大多数大规模数据集是单模态的（仅提供神经影像或遗传数据），缺乏成对的跨模态数据，限制了跨模态交互研究。

Method: CALM框架通过线性投影将两种模态在共享潜空间中对齐，同时匹配类条件潜在分布并确保组间可分性，从而学习可解释的通路-脑区关联。它可以从完全不重叠的人群数据（即不同数据集中分别有影像或遗传数据）进行训练。

Result: 在单模态影像和遗传数据集上训练后，CALM能够泛化到未见过的配对数据集，性能优于多种最先进方法和消融基线；学习到的关联稳定性与配对基线相当。在自闭症谱系障碍实验中，发现了与已有文献一致的免疫和代谢通路与特定皮层区域的关联。

Conclusion: CALM为利用大规模单模态数据仓库研究脑疾病中的跨模态交互打开了大门，能够在不依赖配对数据的情况下从不同数据集中提取有意义的脑-基因关联。

Abstract: The interaction between brain structure and genetic influences is key to understanding neuropsychiatric disorders. However, most large-scale datasets are unimodal, providing either neuroimaging or genetics data. We propose CALM, a framework that learns interpretable associations between brain ROIs and genetic pathways from completely disjoint populations. CALM aligns the two modalities in a shared latent space via linear projections that simultaneously match the class-conditional latent distributions and ensure group separability. These projections provide interpretable pathway--ROI associations. When trained on unimodal imaging and genetics datasets, CALM generalizes to an unseen paired dataset, outperforming several state-of-the-art methods and ablation baselines. We also demonstrate stability of the learned associations against a paired baseline. Our experiments on autism spectrum disorder reveal immune and metabolic pathways linked to specific cortical regions and are consistent with established literature. Thus, CALM opens the door to leveraging large unimodal repositories for studying cross-modal interactions in brain disorders across disparate datasets.

</details>


### [177] [Message Passing Based Two-Timescale Bayesian Learning for Joint Channel and Memory Hardware Impairments Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01660)
*Wei Xu,An Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于残差循环门控单元（RGRU）和消息传递的两时间尺度贝叶斯深度学习框架（MP-TTBDL），用于在大规模MIMO接收机中联合估计信道与硬件损伤，利用快变马尔可夫先验和慢变高斯马尔可夫先验分别建模信道与损伤的不同时间尺度，并通过Turbo-OAMP和DAMP模块迭代交换外信息实现鲁棒的信道估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO接收机中的硬件损伤会引入符号间记忆和阵元间耦合，严重恶化信道估计性能，现有方法未能有效建模损伤的慢变特性与信道的快变特性。

Method: 采用RGRU建模硬件损伤的时隙内记忆，提出MP-TTBDL框架。为信道分配快变马尔可夫先验，为损伤参数分配慢变高斯马尔可夫先验。基于多时隙因子图设计消息传递算法：时隙间消息闭式更新，时隙内因子图分解为信道跟踪模块（Turbo-OAMP进行稀疏估计）和损伤校准模块（DAMP更新损伤参数），两模块通过EP迭代交换外信息直至收敛。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在不同在线损伤场景和SNR条件下，所提框架相比传统补偿器后接信道估计的方法，实现了更低的信道估计误差，且具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的MP-TTBDL框架有效利用了信道与硬件损伤的不同时间尺度特性，通过消息传递与深度学习结合，显著提升了存在硬件损伤时的大规模MIMO信道估计性能。

Abstract: Hardware impairments in massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) receivers introduce inter-symbol memory and inter-element coupling, severely degrading channel estimation. This paper employs a residual recurrent gated unit (RGRU) to model the intra-slot memory of the hardware impairments and proposes a message-passing-based two-timescale Bayesian deep learning (MP-TTBDL) framework for joint channel and impairment tracking. Owing to small-scale fading, the wireless channel varies rapidly across slots, whereas hardware impairments drift slowly due to hardware aging and environmental variations. To capture these distinct physical timescales, a fastvarying Markov prior and a slow-varying Gaussian Markov prior are assigned to the sparse channel and the network parameters, respectively. Based on a multi-slot factor graph formulation, a message-passing algorithm is developed. Specifically, the inter-slot messages admit closed-form updates, while the intra-slot factor graph, due to its complex recurrent structure, is partitioned into a channel tracking module and an impairments calibration module. The channel tracking module performs sparse channel estimation via turbo orthogonal approximate message passing (Turbo-OAMP), and the impairments calibration module updates the impairment parameters via a specially designed deep approximate message passing (DAMP) procedure, with the two modules iteratively exchanging extrinsic information through expectation propagation (EP) until convergence. Simulation results show that the proposed framework robustly achieves lower channel estimation error than conventional compensators followed by channel estimation across different online impairment scenarios and signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions.

</details>


### [178] [Revisiting Decentralized Online Convex Optimization with Compressed Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01665)
*Hao Zhou,Xiaoyu Wang,Chang Yao,Mingli Song,Yuanyu Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出两种FTRL类型的算法用于带压缩通信的分布式在线凸优化，相比OGD类型算法在设计分析和理论界上更优，分别针对全信息设置和bandit设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有D-OCO框架中，精确通信场景下最优算法是FTRL变体，但压缩通信场景下仅存在OGD变体算法，缺乏FTRL类型的方法。

Method: 利用FTRL的对偶更新机制，结合平均共识与通信压缩技术，提出两种FTRL型算法：第一种针对全信息设置，匹配现有遗憾界；第二种针对bandit设置，显著改进遗憾界和通信成本。

Result: 全信息设置下算法能匹配现有遗憾界；bandit设置下算法显著降低了遗憾界和通信开销。

Conclusion: FTRL类型算法在带压缩通信的D-OCO中比OGD类型算法更为优雅高效，尤其bandit场景优势更明显。

Abstract: Decentralized online convex optimization (D-OCO) is a popular framework for distributed applications with streaming data. To tackle the communication bottleneck, previous studies have investigated D-OCO with compressed communication and proposed several algorithms that are variants of online gradient descent (OGD). However, for D-OCO with exact communication, the best existing algorithms are variants of follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL). In this paper, for the first time, we propose two FTRL-type algorithms for D-OCO with compressed communication. Compared with OGD-type algorithms, our algorithms are more elegant in both algorithmic design and theoretical analysis. The key insight is that the dual update mechanism of FTRL allows us to make a simple application of the technique for average consensus with communication compression. More specifically, our first algorithm considers the full-information setting, and can match the existing regret bounds. Our second algorithm is designed for the bandit setting, and can significantly improve both the regret bounds and communication costs of existing algorithms.

</details>


### [179] [UniWind: Toward Unified Day-Ahead Wind Power Forecasting via Physics-Informed State Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01670)
*Ronghui Xu,Tongxin Wu,Guozhen Zhang,Yihan Li,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniWind提出了一种基于物理信息状态路由的风电功率预测模型，通过物理先验估计器、潜在状态编码器和状态感知功率校正器三个模块，在超过20个真实数据集上展示了准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理模型跨风场适应性差，而数据驱动模型容易将气象效应与状态导致的偏差混淆，需要一种能够结合两者优势且能处理运行状态干扰的风电功率预测方法。

Method: UniWind包含三个模块：1) 物理先验估计器(Physical Prior Estimator)，通过站点条件单调扭曲与共享物理功率曲线构建站点校准的物理先验，并施加物理上界约束；2) 潜在状态编码器(Latent State Encoder)，建模运行状态嵌入；3) 状态感知功率校正器(State-aware Power Corrector)，通过知识引导的有监督状态路由和有界状态特定专家校正，将物理先验转换为最终功率预测。

Result: 在超过20个真实世界数据集上的全样本和跨风场零样本实验中，UniWind展示了较高的预测准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UniWind通过物理信息状态路由有效融合了物理模型与数据驱动模型的优势，能够处理实际风电场中的运行状态干扰，实现了准确且跨场泛化的风电功率预测。

Abstract: Day-ahead wind power forecasting is essential for cost-effective power-system operation. It is primarily driven by future meteorological conditions while retaining temporal dependencies in power generation. In practice, observed wind-farm power often entangles physically available power with local environmental effects and latent operational states, such as shutdowns and curtailment. Existing physical models provide useful constraints but adapt poorly across wind farms, whereas data-driven models can capture rich correlations but often conflate meteorological effects with state-induced deviations. In this study, we propose UniWind, a wind power forecasting model based on physics-informed state routing. UniWind first employs a Physical Prior Estimator to construct a site-calibrated physical prior by combining site-conditioned monotonic warping with a shared physical power curve. It further applies a physical upper-bound constraint to shape this prior as a soft envelope of available wind power generation. UniWind then proposes a Latent State Encoder to model operating-state embeddings and transforms the physical prior into final power forecasts through a State-aware Power Corrector, which uses knowledge-guided supervised state routing and bounded, state-specific expert correction. Full-shot and cross-farm zero-shot experiments on more than 20 real-world datasets demonstrate the accuracy and robustness of UniWind.

</details>


### [180] [SCAPE: Accurate and Efficient LLM Training with Extreme Sparse Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01678)
*Mingkai Zheng,Junlin Chen,Haotian Xie,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCAPE是一种面向LLM训练的高效通信分布式优化器，通过基于一阶动量的激进稀疏化技术，在90%-99%稀疏度下保持模型质量，并将预训练时间缩短最高43.3%，每步加速达3.26倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中通信开销日益主导成本，尤其在数据并行和分片训练下，梯度同步和参数重建开销随模型规模和系统规模增长。现有方法如梯度稀疏化对Adam类优化器不稳定，量化通信则受限于位宽且产生额外运行时开销。

Method: 利用AdamS一阶矩的稳定性实现激进稀疏化：从一阶矩统计量构建掩码而非原始梯度；跨工作节点分区生成掩码以对齐优化器分片；延迟一步使用掩码使同步与计算重叠；从单一稀疏缓冲区重建二阶矩更新所需量，避免额外集合通信。在Megatron-LM中实现。

Result: 在32×GH200 GPU上预训练GPT-345M和Llama-500M，90%-99%稀疏度下保持训练稳定性和验证损失；Llama-500M端到端预训练时间减少43.3%；Llama-1.8B每步加速达3.26倍。

Conclusion: SCAPE能在极高稀疏度下保持与密集AdamW/AdamS相当的质量，同时大幅降低通信开销和训练时间，是一种实用的LLM训练加速方案。

Abstract: Communication increasingly dominates the cost of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training, especially under data-parallel and sharded training schemes, where gradient synchronization and parameter reconstruction overhead increase with model size and system scale. Existing communication-reduction methods either sparsify raw gradients, which can be unstable for modern Adam-style optimizers at high sparsity, or quantize communication, whose savings are fundamentally bounded by bit width and often incur additional runtime overhead. We present SCAPE, a communication-efficient distributed optimizer for LLM training that exploits the stability of AdamS's first-moment to enable aggressive sparsification without loss of LLM quality. Instead of constructing masks from raw gradients, SCAPE derives them from first-moment-based statistics, partitions mask generation across workers to align with optimizer sharding, and delays mask usage by one step so that mask synchronization can overlap with computation. SCAPE also reconstructs the quantities required for second-moment updates from a single synchronized sparse buffer, avoiding an additional collective. We implement SCAPE in Megatron-LM and evaluate its convergence by pre-training GPT-345M on OpenWebText and Llama-500M on SlimPajama-6B using 32 NVIDIA GH200 GPUs on TACC Vista. In both models, SCAPE preserves training stability, validation loss, and downstream task accuracy under 90\% and 99\% sparsity. For Llama-500M, SCAPE reduces end-to-end pre-training wall-clock time by up to 43.3\% while maintaining model quality comparable to dense AdamW and AdamS. For Llama-1.8B, SCAPE achieves up to 3.26$\times$ speedup per step compared to dense AdamS.

</details>


### [181] [WARP: Weight-Space Analysis for Recovering Training Data Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01686)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Aditya Goyal,John Cooper,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARP通过模型合并生成伪检查点，从微调模型的权重中恢复其训练数据的领域混合比例，在BERT和GPT-2上平均MAE分别低至0.046和0.104，优于成员推理等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型公开发布时，其训练数据的领域混合权重（即不同数据源的采样比例）几乎从不披露，导致研究人员能研究模型却无法了解其训练分布，形成信息不对称。

Method: WARP利用模型合并技术，在基座模型与微调模型之间插值生成伪检查点，模拟缺失的训练轨迹；从这些伪检查点提取几何特征，通过无参数softmax读出器或基于合成混合训练的MLP投影器将特征映射为领域比例。

Result: 在BERT和GPT-2的受控实验中，WARP恢复领域混合的平均MAE分别低至0.046和0.104，优于成员推理方法以及能访问真实训练轨迹的变体方法。

Conclusion: WARP有效揭示了微调模型训练数据的全局组成，为研究训练分布提供了新工具，缓解了因数据配方不透明造成的分析不对称问题。

Abstract: Foundation models are routinely released to the public, yet the data recipes used to train them -- such as domain mixture weights that determine how different sources are sampled -- are rarely disclosed. This creates an access asymmetry: researchers study the resulting models but lack visibility into the training distribution that produces them. Prior works for inferring training data, such as membership inference, detect at the level of individual samples and thus cannot characterize the global composition of the training corpus. We introduce WARP, a framework that recovers a fine-tuned model's training mixtures directly from its released weights. WARP interpolates between the base and fine-tuned models using model merging, generating pseudo-checkpoints that approximate the missing training trajectory and expose a geometric footprint of the training data in the weight space. From these simulated footprints, WARP extracts geometric features and maps them to domain proportions using either a parameter-free softmax readout or an MLP projector trained on synthetic mixtures. In controlled experiments with BERT and GPT-2, WARP recovers domain mixtures with an average MAE as low as 0.046 and 0.104 respectively, outperforming membership inference and a variant with access to the true training trajectory.

</details>


### [182] [Model Merging as Probabilistic Inference in Fine-Tuning Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01689)
*Long Minh Bui,Tuan Anh Le Van,Tung Phi Duc,Phi Le Nguyen,Jana Doppa,Trong Nghia Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从概率推断的新视角（乘积专家模型）重新审视模型合并问题，指出现有方法隐含高斯假设与真实残差的重尾分布不匹配，提出基于柯西专家的重尾PoE设计，在多项任务和架构上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法主要依赖局部解空间的几何特性，但几何视角无法有效评估各任务特定更新方向在合并过程中的统计有用性。

Method: 将模型合并形式化为乘积专家（PoE）场景下的概率推断问题：每个单任务解定义了一个基于能量的专家模型（EBM）；发现现有方法的隐式高斯假设与重尾残差不匹配，提出基于柯西专家的重尾PoE设计，并保证推理过程的收敛性。

Result: 揭示了现有模型合并方法与真实残差分布之间的不匹配（高斯vs重尾）；提出的重尾柯西PoE方法在多项任务和架构上显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 模型合并可以从概率推断视角统一理解；采用更适合数据分布的分布假设（如重尾）能有效提升合并性能。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine existing single-task solutions into a multi-task solution without additional data-driven fine-tuning.~Most existing approaches achieve this using geometric properties of local solution spaces. However, such geometric views provide limited guidance for scoring how statistically useful each task-specific update direction is across tasks during merging. To address this, we formulate model merging from a new perspective of probabilistic inference under a product-of-experts (PoE) scenario where each single-task solution defines an energy-based expert model (EBM) over the merged parameters. We show that several existing model merging methods arise as special cases of our framework under energy designs that impose implicit Gaussian assumptions on directional residuals between merged and task-specific models. Empirically, we find that these residuals are often heavy-tailed which exposes a mismatch with the imposed light-tailed Gaussian structures. We address this with a heavy-tailed PoE design based on Cauchy experts, which better captures the observed residual behavior while admitting a provably convergent inference procedure. Experiments across multiple tasks and architectures show significant improvements over state-of-the-arts baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/MinhLong210/PoE-EBM-Merging.git.

</details>


### [183] [A Mathematical Introduction to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01693)
*Jianfeng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从采样视角对扩散模型进行了面向证明的导论性介绍，涵盖了从经典采样动力学到现代扩散采样器、误差分析及推理时控制的完整脉络，适合具备概率论基础但无随机微分方程或扩散模型背景的研究生初学者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为具备概率论基础但缺乏随机微分方程、随机数值或扩散模型背景的研究生初学者提供一套从经典采样动力学到现代扩散采样器的系统性、证明导向的入门教材，填补该领域在严谨推导与教学层面的空白。

Method: 采用分层递进的结构：核心定义与恒等式给予完整证明；代表性估计在简化假设下证明；研究级定理给出证明路线图。内容从经典采样动力学出发，经现代扩散采样器，涵盖误差分析与推理时控制。

Result: 构建了一套从经典采样动力学到现代扩散采样器的完整理论框架，提供了核心恒等式的完整证明、代表性估计的简化证明以及研究级定理的证明路线图，形成系统化的教学材料。

Conclusion: 该笔记为研究生初学者提供了一个从采样视角理解扩散模型的严谨、自洽的入门路径，通过分层证明策略降低了学习难度，同时保持了理论的完整性。

Abstract: These notes give a proof-oriented introduction to diffusion models from the viewpoint of sampling, tracing a single arc from classical sampling dynamics to modern diffusion samplers, their error analysis, and inference-time control. Throughout, the material is layered into core definitions and identities proved in full, representative estimates proved under simplifying assumptions, and research-level theorems stated with a proof roadmap. The intended audience is beginning graduate students with a background in probability but no prior exposure to stochastic differential equations, stochastic numerics, or diffusion models.

</details>


### [184] [Frequency Shift Physics-Informed Extreme Learning Machine for Solving High-Frequency Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01694)
*Xiong Xiong,Ruonan Zhai,Zheng Zeng,Sheng Zhou,Rongchun Hu,Zichen Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频率移位物理信息极限学习机（FS-PIELM），通过加法权重初始化机制解决高频率PDE求解中的谱偏差问题，在七个基准测试中相比现有方法提升1-5个数量级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息机器学习中，神经网络求解高频率偏微分方程时存在谱偏差（优先学习低频成分），导致高频率解精度低下，现有缩放方法会放大方差，亟需一种能保持方差可控且有效覆盖高频的初始化机制。

Method: 提出FS-PIELM框架：采用加法机制初始化权重，将高斯权重分布的均值平移至目标频率，方差固定为1避免放大；开发两个变体——FS-PIELM-L（每个神经元独立频率）和FS-PIELM-G（神经元分组增强鲁棒性）；理论证明频率方差有界且趋近于1；仅需单次线性求解保持极限学习机计算效率。

Result: 在Helmholtz、波动、Poisson、Klein-Gordon、热传导、对流扩散等六类方程、七个基准问题上，FS-PIELM-L在六项中取得最优精度，相比现有PIELM变体提升1到近5个数量级。

Conclusion: FS-PIELM通过加法频率移位有效克服了谱偏差，在不牺牲极限学习机计算效率的前提下显著提升了高频率PDE求解精度，为物理信息机器学习处理高频问题提供了新的初始化范式。

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency solutions remains a central challenge in physics-informed machine learning due to spectral bias -- the tendency of neural networks to learn low-frequency components preferentially. This paper proposes a Frequency Shift Physics-Informed Extreme Learning Machine (FS-PIELM) framework that addresses this limitation through an additive mechanism for weight initialization. Rather than multiplying random weights by a scaling factor, the method translates the mean of the Gaussian weight distribution while keeping the variance fixed at unity, thereby avoiding the variance amplification inherent in scaling-based methods. Two variants are developed: FS-PIELM-L assigns independent frequency magnitudes to individual neurons, while FS-PIELM-G groups neurons for improved robustness. Theoretical analysis shows that the frequency variance under the proposed framework remains bounded and approaches unity regardless of target frequency, in contrast to the quadratic growth of conventional approaches. The method preserves the computational efficiency of extreme learning machines, requiring only a single linear solve.
